# SP78_C13_147: changes against the two witnesses

Only substantive readings are counted. A row may differ from both witnesses and then contributes to both witness totals. Macro wrappers alone and certainty-only refinements are excluded, including reordering or narrowing pre-existing candidates while the reading remains unresolved. Resolutions of indexed doubts are separately recorded in `decisions.tsv`.

## Consequential corrections

- Scan 054, physical line 1, dimension formula: `\dim\operatorname{amb}G=\nu` / `\operatorname{dimmod}G=\nu` → `\dim\operatorname{mod}G=\nu`. After dim, the suffix has compressed low m/o strokes followed by a separate rising d with a short descending return. It has neither the initial rounded a nor the looped final b visible in amb in the three lower formulas.
- Scan 054, display (65), third row dimension formula: `\nu=2-\dim\operatorname{amb}G` / `\nu=2=\dim\operatorname{amb}G` → `\nu=2-\dim\operatorname{amb}G`. Between 2 and dim there is one dark horizontal stroke. A much paler short ghost lies below it, unlike both dark parallel strokes of the preceding equals sign. The dark ink supports the single minus kept by C; no second dark bar is supplied.
- Scan 056, display (68), closing parentheses around the two alternatives: `a close parenthesis after poids marqué 1 and a close parenthesis after the set of pairs` / `one large outer closing parenthesis after both alternatives, with the second alternative nested` → `a close parenthesis after poids marqué 1 and a close parenthesis after the set of pairs`. A curved right parenthesis immediately follows the numeral 1 on the second line. A fresh left parenthesis precedes the genus-zero alternative; its matching right parenthesis is on the last line. Thus the two cases have separate parentheses.
- Scan 058, rotated margin, final dimension: `2.` / `\uncertain{1, 2}` → `2.`. The final line ends with one rounded upper-turn digit 2, a horizontal foot and a distinct period. There is no preceding 1 or internal comma.
- Scan 058, first curve, distribution of a,b,c,d,e over the two branches: `a,b on one branch; c,d,e on the other` / `table: (a,b,c; d,e); drawing description: a,b / c,d,e` → `a,b on the descending branch; c,d,e on the ascending branch`. H’s prose drawing description agrees, but its tabular grouping does not.

### Unresolved places

- Punctuation after top in the insertion.
- Full readings of canceled Et, il n’y a pas, and qui.
- Final s of sommets in line 7.
- Presence of an abbreviation point after inserted isom.
- ou versus a dash between the count values 1 and 2.
- The reduced mark after the first homéom. remains à/colon.
- The parenthetical phrase after il doit now reads doubtful en avoir; its overworked opening is not fully separable.

- M1035: U versus double-stroked P remains open; B is not supported.
- M1038: denominator remains \unsure{24|6}; no separately readable canceled digit is added.
- Alphabet of the three ornate X occurrences remains calligraphic versus fraktur.
- Unindexed canceled hat/exclamation above M remains doubtful under hatching.
- Added doubt on canceled schémas because the strike masks parts of its internal letters.
- First t subscript1/4, heavily retraced.
- Numérique: compressed final letters at right edge.

- M1051 remains correspondant|correspondent; the order records preference, not resolution.
- The added upper NB canceled cluster remains a doubtful deux.
- The separate lower NB canceled cluster remains a doubtful deux.
- The far-right en has unresolved attachment between the slanted margin rows.
- M1052 remains des deux faces under doubt.
- The main-text deux may be canceled or may remain under the l'un des insertion connector; both state/order readings stand.
- The crossed fragment before 2 remains côt under doubt.
- The mark after correspond remains à|:.

### Unresolved places

- Inserted ending déployé versus déployée; déployé preferred without excluding the second e.
- M1055 et tout versus et tant; neither reading is ruled out.
- M1056 va remains unresolved.
- The entire canceled M_{0,I} remains doubtful despite the I-shaped final index.
- Canceled stable and tout retain uncertainty because strokes cover their letters.
- The final delimiter after combinatoire remains a closing parenthesis versus square bracket; the preferred parenthesis is explicitly qualified.

It does not securely distinguish a closing parenthesis from a square bracket. ### Unresolved places

- M1058 remains undecided; the M exponent must not be inferred from the clear bang on Gamma.
- The heading's canceled exponent and brace-like mark, canceled hat-M group, and two words of the long bottom strike remain doubtful.
- The choice numeral 1 may have an underline merged with its heavy foot; both readings are retained.
- The diagram's common point P_1 and the prose's common point P_0 remain inconsistent as written.
- The lowered Z attachment in U_{0,3 Z} is preferred at medium confidence; the visible 3 remains unchanged.
### Independent second reading

Independent reader audit_b wrote 10 box readings before comparison. The token comparison found 9 differing boxes. The main reader examined each on its crop; 2 boxes required a transcription change. Equivalent TeX encodings and retained uncertainties are explained in the box-level adjudications.

### Unresolved places

- Opening struck se déploie is still a candidate.
- The inline J definition remains J-prime followed by colon versus J with a raised exclamation; the detached low point is definite, but its grouping is unresolved.
- The first display label remains 84|83.
- All three canceled symmetric-group trials remain doubtful, including possible leading punctuation in (85).
- M1060 retains uncertain barred-epsilon-like trailing material and exact subscript grouping.
- The pair beside inserted P0 remains :|;|à.
- The significance of the sole section T=2 in (87), against the two branch values of the displayed rational map, remains for later mathematical review; the source section is preserved.

### Unresolved places

- First boundary-M accent: canceled/overworked hat versus live tilde.
- Inserted M_{g,I}: wedge coincides with an upper f-descender; hat versus bare M remains doubtful.
- Upper dimension formula lacks a separable opening parenthesis; both literal and supplied-parenthesis readings are shown.
- M1064 coefficient 2/3 and lower opening parenthesis remain unresolved.
- Underlying g' in the canceled diagram patch is only a tentative suggestion.
- Final digit of the first canceled inequality is overlapped by a live inserted descender and remains doubtful.
- The literal g'+g''=0 is retained despite the generic weighted-genus expectation.
### Independent second reading

Independent reader audit_b wrote 14 box readings before comparison. The token comparison found 11 differing boxes. The main reader examined each on its crop; 2 boxes required a transcription change. Equivalent TeX encodings and retained uncertainties are explained in the box-level adjudications.

### Unresolved places

- Identity/state of the upper b-like glyph above live a-prime.
- Canceled quelconque, whole on-a/phi/card trial, si on suppose, avec, OPS, a il y a, pour and qui a donc remain doubtful.
- New preliminary g and the lower middle-sketch g-like glyph remain doubtful under their crossings.
- Semicolon/comma after canceled ≥3 remains unresolved.
- Right caption keeps minus/plus uncertainty and an overwritten I-double-prime doubt.
- The geometric specialization claims are not proved by the limited algebra checks.
- The live noun notations remains preferred under uncertainty because its compressed internal letters are not individually separable.
- The first canceled comparison between card I-prime and card I-double-prime remains ≥ versus >; the later ≥3 is separately readable.

### Unresolved places

- M1070: shape and state of the Gamma superscript (!, natural-like form, or possibly canceled !).
- Initial overwork in s’insère; live state preferred but its letters/possible partial cancellation are not fully separable.
- Canceled par: p is visible but the a/r bodies remain merged with the strike.
- Quotient: the present candidate is retained, but its overworked ending cannot be fully separated.
- The first canceled de candidate has an obscured ending and remains qualified.
### Independent second reading

Independent reader audit_b wrote 14 box readings before comparison. The token comparison found 13 differing boxes. The main reader examined each on its crop; 1 boxes required a transcription change. Equivalent TeX encodings and retained uncertainties are explained in the box-level adjudications.

|---|---|---|---|---|
| B07 | \unsure{s'insère} \deleted{de \unsure{par}} \inserted{dans} une structure d'extension | \unsure{s\textquotesingle insère} \deleted{\unsure{dans} \unsure{par}} \inserted{dans une} structure d\textquotesingle extension | \unsure{s'insère} \deleted{\unsure{de} \unsure{par}} \inserted{dans une} structure d'extension | The first canceled cluster begins with a d loop, but the thick horizontal strike merges its low ending, so de becomes a qualified candidate. Par remains doubtful under the second strike. Dans and une occupy the raised row directly above the two canceled clusters; extend the insertion to both words. The following structure d’extension continues from that raised row. S’insère remains the existing uncertain live candidate. |

- M1073 et ce sera remains uncertain beneath a crossing line.
- The Gamma bang in the third margin is retraced and remains qualified.

### Unresolved places

- The crossed second opening roof, quoted l’infini/l’âme, whole de la réunion/sur le germe group, and other formulas/words inside the large cancellation remain doubtful.
- The slanted trial and non seuls stay provisional under the strike; the inserted M hat is clear but its G underline is uncertain.
- Both raised signs in (100) remain star/left-arrow alternatives; the absence of independent M roofs is resolved.
- The first repeated ou is reduced; du connexe remains unresolved.
- Both canceled diagram groups remain provisional; the extra closing curve has uncertain attachment.
- The final reduced à/colon mark remains unresolved. No following-page completion is supplied.
- The live prefix Ils sont donc is retained with whole-group uncertainty.
